<h2>Also filed in other trusts</h2>
<p>Loans are sometimes split across trusts (pari passu), and each trust files the loan at its whole-loan amount. A loan with the same property name and origination date is filed in: <a href="/cmbs/loan/benchmark-2018-b7-mortgage-trust--10/">Benchmark 2018-B7 Mortgage Trust</a>; <a href="/cmbs/loan/cd-2018-cd7-mortgage-trust-d03--10/">CD 2018-CD7 Mortgage Trust (segment D03, CIK 1746406)</a>.</p>
